- **clarify-writing**: Write or rewrite English so that a person or an agent cannot misread it. Applies Simplified Technical English (ASD-STE100) rules and a scan for AI writing tells. Use for all writing: docs, PR descriptions, commit messages, reports, chat replies, skills, prompts, agent briefs, tool descriptions, error messages and status text. - **clarifying**: 一次一题把一个想法、计划或决定问清楚（grill 式）：能自己查到的事实不问用户，需要用户拿主意的逐题问并附推荐。用户要求逐题追问、把某个想法/计划/决定磨清楚时使用；也被 exploring、requirement-analysis、quick-fix 和 visual-preview 引用。开放式的思考陪伴用 exploring；已决定要做的开发工作直接用 requirement-analysis 或 quick-fix。 ([FlameMida/spec-dev](https://github.com/FlameMida/spec-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/clarifying) · ★12)
- **clarifying**: Plain-words interview that turns a rough app idea into a shared picture, deciding technical details itself and asking only about what people see, do, and pay for. - **clarifying-assumptions**: Runs the conversational clarification layer for workflow orchestration. - **clarifying-contracts**: Resolve user-authoritative semantic ambiguity after inspection identifies an unresolved choice that could change purpose, direct object, public behavior, constraints, invariants, scope, decision authority, or acceptance evidence, or when the user asks to clarify or stress-test such a contract. - **clarifying-features**: Settles what a feature must do before any plan — from whatever sources exist, asking the user only what no source can answer — and writes PLAN.md's spec sections. Use when the user says "plan this feature", "new feature", "here is the user story" or "build this screen" with a design link, or pastes a title-only ticket or rough notes. For "write the plan", use planning-features; for "implement the plan", executing-plans; for a root cause, investigating-bugs. ([lidominik02/frontend-devkit](https://github.com/lidominik02/frontend-devkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/core/skills/clarifying-features) · ★1)
- **clarifying-intent**: Settles ambiguous user intent or an unanswered decision before work proceeds. Skip inputs another skill's own procedure asks for. ([augments-labs/sdlc-skills](https://github.com/augments-labs/sdlc-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-skills/skills/clarifying-intent) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **clarifying-intent**: Intent-discovery interview before building anything new or changing behavior. Use when a request names a thing to build but not who it is for, why, or what done means, when the user asks to be grilled or to stress-test an idea, or before any feature, component, or behavior change whose purpose and success criteria are not yet agreed. ([shreyansh-jain/agent-maxxing](https://github.com/shreyansh-jain/agent-maxxing/tree/HEAD/skills/clarifying-intent))
- **clarifying-interview**: This skill should be used when the user is designing, planning, architecting, or scoping something new — e.g. "let's build X", "design X", "plan X", "how should I structure X", "I want to add X", or before entering plan mode.
